Use this skill when a user wants a field-level biomarker evidence map rather than a generic literature summary. Always separate exploratory biomarkers from externally validated or clinically embedded biomarkers, and never imply clinical maturity without explicit evidence support. ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/awesome-med-research-skills/Evidence%20Insight/biomarker-landscape-scanner) · ★2.1k)
- **biomarker-literature-curation**: Decision support for testing whether restricting raw LC-MS metabolomic input to literature-reported biomarker m/z windows (3 ppm, PC&gt;1/3/8) lowers a trained classifier's accuracy versus the full raw-data baseline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biomarker-literature-curation))
- **biomarker-log-transformation-and-scaling**: Use when preparing Nightingale-platform metabolic biomarker concentration tables (with skewness and cohort-specific distributions) for Cox proportional hazards models or meta-analysis, especially when zeroes and cross-cohort scale differences are present. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biomarker-log-transformation-and-scaling))
- **biomarker-metabolite-module-mining**: Use when you have a cleaned metabolite abundance matrix (e.g. tidyMass peak-area feature table) spanning many samples and need to group metabolites into co-accumulation modules and nominate hub metabolites as biomarker candidates when traditional group-based DAM comparison is impractical. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biomarker-metabolite-module-mining))
- **biomarker-missingness-handling**: Use when post-QC, log-scaled UK Biobank NMR biomarker concentrations still contain plate-level shifts attributable to unexplained technical causes, and outlier shipment plates must be detected and their samples set to missing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biomarker-missingness-handling))
- **biomarker-multi-agent-discovery**: Use when orchestrating a multi-agent biomarker discovery workflow that requires coordinating database queries, pathway analysis, literature review, statistical modeling, and clinical evidence synthesis to produce ranked biomarker panels. ([aws-samples/amazon-bedrock-agents-healthcare-lifesciences](https://github.com/aws-samples/amazon-bedrock-agents-healthcare-lifesciences/tree/HEAD/skills/biomarker-multi-agent-discovery) · ★277)
- **biomarker-novelty-classification**: Use when IC gene symbol lists from the fused co-expression pipeline must be triaged into drug-actionable versus literature-novel candidate biomarkers per cancer type (KIRC, LIHC, PRAD). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/biomarker-novelty-classification))
- **biomarker-panel-prioritization**: Use when a muSignAl run yields many candidate biomarker signatures (e.g. 158 panels from Olink CVD-II in sign_cvd2.xlsx) and you must rank or threshold them by AUC to shortlist panels worth developing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/biomarker-panel-prioritization))
- **biomarker-pathway-analysis**: Use when a researcher needs to analyze biological pathways for biomarker discovery, map disease mechanisms to druggable targets using Reactome/KEGG, identify pathway enrichment from gene sets, or understand mechanism-of-action for candidate biomarkers. ([aws-samples/amazon-bedrock-agents-healthcare-lifesciences](https://github.com/aws-samples/amazon-bedrock-agents-healthcare-lifesciences/tree/HEAD/skills/biomarker-pathway-analysis) · ★277)
- **biomarker-pipeline**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Workflow_Management/workflows/biomarker-pipeline) · ★7)

([MaintainTechnology/humansoferp](https://github.com/MaintainTechnology/humansoferp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/biomarker-pipeline) · 6 copies)
- **biomarker-predictive-performance-evaluation**: Use when you have continuous biomarker scores for multiple immunotherapy cohorts with a binary response column ('res') and need to compare predictive performance (ROC/AUC) of the new biomarker against existing markers like PD-L1 or TMB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/biomarker-predictive-performance-evaluation))
- **biomarker-recovery-verification-against-supplementary-figures**: Use when a clustering method claims to support downstream DEG analysis and you must verify that recovered marker genes match those reported in the paper's supplementary figures. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/biomarker-recovery-verification-against-supplementary-figures))
- **biomarker-rejection-count-validation**: Use when many metabolite hypotheses are tested simultaneously and FDR inflation must be reduced via a two-dimensional local fdr rule based on the t statistic (z1) and the log standard error of the test statistic (z2). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biomarker-rejection-count-validation))
- **biomarker-sidecar-draft**: Draft `BiomarkerActionability` (BMA-*) and `Biomarker` (BIO-*) sidecar candidates for OpenOnco maintainer review from manifest-owned entities only. Use when the user asks to draft BMA/BIO sidecars or is on a chunk whose manifest includes BMA/BIO entities. Drafts only — neutral evidence wording, no treatment recommendations, no patient-specific output, no edits to hosted clinical content. Banned sources for the OpenOnco pilot are OncoKB, SNOMED CT, and MedDRA — never cite these in `evidence_sources` or `primary_sources` regardless of how the user frames the request. ([romeo111/task_torrent](https://github.com/romeo111/task_torrent/tree/HEAD/plugins/openonco-contributor/skills/biomarker-sidecar-draft))
- **biomarker-signature-thresholding**: Use when building an omic biomarker signature from a feature/sample matrix and you need a stability-based cutoff (feature frequency across resampled models) rather than a single-model coefficient ranking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/biomarker-signature-thresholding))
- **biomarker-strategy**: Develop biomarker strategies for drug development. TRIGGERS - Use when user needs help with biomarker-strategy related tasks. ([lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection](https://github.com/lionelsimai/claude-skills-collection/tree/HEAD/skills/biomarker-strategy) · ★39 · 2 copies)
- **biomarker-threshold-cell-classification**: Use when evaluating a marker-gene thresholding baseline for cell-type mapping: target cells must be assigned to reference clusters using top cluster markers alone, and the Classified/Ambiguous/Orphan fractions must be quantified. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/biomarker-threshold-cell-classification))

Combines 4 tools from 4 SCP server(s). ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/biomarker_discovery) · ★399 · 3 copies)

- **biomass-gasifier-sizing**: Quantitative sizing of a downdraft biomass gasifier for small-scale power (rice husk / wood chips, ~5-50 kWe). Use for reactor throat/hearth diameters, hearth & throat load, air/fuel & equivalence ratio (ER), syngas LHV, cold-gas efficiency (CGE), fuel-rate ↔ electric-power chain, and sanity ranges. Triggers on "gasifier design", "khí hóa trấu/sinh khối", "throat diameter", "hearth load", "syngas LHV/ER/CGE", "biomass to electricity sizing". ([pvbang/nexus](https://github.com/pvbang/nexus/tree/HEAD/skills/biomass-gasifier-sizing))
- **biomass-plant-technician**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a Biomass Plant Technician — adjusting combustion parameters in response to actual measured fuel moisture content rather than a fixed setpoint, diagnosing a fuel feed interruption as mechanical bridging rather than a combustion issue, adjusting soot-blowing frequency to actual observed slagging conditions rather than a fixed calendar schedule, or monitoring emissions more actively given fuel batch variability rather than a "set and monitor occasionally" approach appropriate for consistent fossil fuel. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/biomass-plant-technician) · ★27)
- **biomass-power-plant-manager**: Use when a task needs the judgment of a Biomass Power Plant Manager — running a biomass-fired power generation facility, managing fuel supply logistics and quality variability, evaluating combustion/efficiency problems, or balancing generation output against fuel cost and emissions constraints. ([wonsukchoi/domain-experts](https://github.com/wonsukchoi/domain-experts/tree/HEAD/roles/biomass-power-plant-manager) · ★27)
- **biomass-reduction-heatmap-visualization**: Use when gap-filled community GEMs (e.g. COBRA workspaces from run_iterative_gap_filling.m) are available and you need to identify which exchanged metabolites each member depends on and visualize the resulting dependency matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/biomass-reduction-heatmap-visualization))
- **biomass-trend-significance-testing**: Use when you have qPCR-derived total microbial abundance estimates for paired samples (e.g., day 27 vs day 362 anaerobic digester biomass) and need to test temporal trends before and after GCN correction.
